Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The extremal operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 324. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 949 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_324(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 324. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^949(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.4 (Invariance Principle)
